## Description

#### What type of PR is this?

/kind documentation
/area readme

#### What this PR does / why we need it?
    
Turns the README into a short landing page and moves the reference material into collapsible sections, summary:

| | Before | After | Change |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Words visible before expanding | 887 | 348 | −61% |
| Links visible before expanding | 110 | 32 | −71% |
| Page height on desktop, collapsed | 5,118 px | 1,684 px | −67% |
| Page height on mobile, collapsed | 6,563 px | 2,332 px | −64% |
| Deepest list nesting | 5 levels | 2 levels | −3 levels |
| ✔️ marks and other emoji | 62 | 0 | −62 |
| Unique links | 110 | 146 | all kept, +36 |


#### Useful notes for your reviewer

- **Rendered preview:** https://github.com/mimowo/kueue/blob/refresh-landing-readme/README.md
- **Nothing is lost:** every link in the current README is still there, including all 38 TestGrid dashboards and 21 roadmap issues. Only the two badge images are gone. The 33 new links point to per-integration pages, setup guides and concept pages.
- **Roadmap:** the same priorities and issues, reworded as some of the issues are already shipped. 
- **Kubernetes version:** now "1.34 or newer is recommended; older versions may work but aren't tested", matching the installation guide.

AI disclosure: I used AI assistants to draft and review this change, and reviewed the result myself in multiple iterations.

#### Release note

```release-note
NONE
```

## AI summary

The README is now a shorter landing page with capability highlights, getting-started guidance, and links to project resources. It reorganizes integration, roadmap, production-readiness, and compatibility information. It recommends Kubernetes 1.34 or newer and notes that older versions may work but are not tested.

### Suggested release note

`NONE` — This documentation change does not alter Kueue behavior.

-# Kueue
-
-[![Latest Release](https://img.shields.io/github/v/release/kubernetes-sigs/kueue?include_prereleases)](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/releases/latest)
-[![Ask DeepWiki](https://deepwiki.com/badge.svg)](https://deepwiki.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue)
-
-<img src="https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/blob/main/site/static/images/logo.svg" width="100" alt="kueue logo">
-
-Kueue is a set of APIs and controller for [job](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload)
-[queueing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts#queueing). It is a job-level manager that decides when
-a job should be [admitted](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts#admission) to start (as in pods can be
-created) and when it should stop (as in active pods should be deleted).
-
-Read the [overview](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/overview/) and watch the Kueue-related [talks & presentations](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/community/talks_and_presentations/) to learn more.
-
-You can also [help Kueue grow](#help-kueue-grow).
-
-## Features overview
-
-- **Job management:** Support job queueing based on [priorities](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload/#priority) with different [strategies](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#queueing-strategy): `StrictFIFO` and `BestEffortFIFO`.
-- **Advanced Resource management:** Comprising: [resource flavor fungibility](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#flavorfungibility), [Fair Sharing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/preemption/#fair-sharing), [cohorts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#cohort) and [preemption](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#preemption) with a variety of policies between different tenants.
-- **Integrations:** Built-in support for popular jobs, e.g. [BatchJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/), [Kubeflow training jobs](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/), [RayJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayjobs/), [RayCluster](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayclusters/), [JobSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobsets/),  [plain Pod and Pod Groups](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/plain_pods/).
-- **System insight:** Built-in [prometheus metrics](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/reference/metrics/) to help monitor the state of the system, and on-demand visibility endpoint for [monitoring of pending workloads](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/manage/monitor_pending_workloads/pending_workloads_on_demand/).
-- **AdmissionChecks:** A mechanism for internal or external components to influence whether a workload can be [admitted](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/).
-- **Advanced autoscaling support:** Integration with cluster-autoscaler's [provisioningRequest](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/provisioning_request/#job-using-a-provisioningrequest) via admissionChecks.
-- **All-or-nothing with ready Pods:** A timeout-based implementation of [All-or-nothing scheduling](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/manage/setup_wait_for_pods_ready/).
-- **Partial admission and dynamic reclaim:** mechanisms to run a job with [reduced parallelism](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/#partial-admission), based on available quota, and to [release](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload/#dynamic-reclaim) the quota the pods complete.
-- **Mixing training and inference**: Simultaneous management of batch workloads along with serving workloads (such as [Deployments](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/deployment/) or [StatefulSets](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/statefulset/))
-- **Multi-cluster job dispatching:** called [MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/), allows to search for capacity and off-load the main cluster.
-- **Topology-Aware Scheduling**: Allows to optimize the Pod-to-Pod communication throughput by [scheduling aware of the data-center topology](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/topology_aware_scheduling/).
-- **Dynamic Resource Allocation (DRA):** Quota management for devices such as GPUs requested through [DRA](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/dynamic_resource_allocation/), either with ResourceClaimTemplates or extended resources, including partitionable devices. Consumable capacity is available as an alpha feature.
-

-## Installation
-
-**Officially supported and tested on Kubernetes 1.34 or newer**.
-
-To install the latest release of Kueue in your cluster, run the following command:
+<h1 align="center">
+  <img src="site/static/images/logo.svg" alt="Kueue logo" width="40" height="40"> Kueue
+</h1>
 
-```shell
-kubectl apply --server-side -f https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/releases/download/v0.20.3/manifests.yaml
-```
+<p align="center">
+  <strong><a href="#get-started">Get&nbsp;started</a></strong> ·
+  <a href="https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/">Docs</a> ·
+  <a href="#community">Community</a> ·
+  <a href="#help-kueue-grow">Help&nbsp;Kueue&nbsp;grow</a>
+</p>
 
-The controller runs in the `kueue-system` namespace.
+Kueue is a Kubernetes-native queueing and scheduling system that decides **when**, **where** and **how** your training, inference and batch workloads run.
 
-Read the [installation guide](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/installation/) to learn more.
+It works alongside kube-scheduler, cluster-autoscaler and your job controllers instead of replacing them.
+Learn more in the [overview](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/overview/).
 
-## Usage
+## Capabilities at a glance
 
-A minimal configuration can be set by running the [examples](site/static/examples):
+- **[Fair sharing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/fair_sharing/)** — share quota fairly across teams, with borrowing through [cohorts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cohort/) and optional preemption.
+- **[Topology-Aware Scheduling (TAS)](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/topology_aware_scheduling/)** — place communicating Pods close together, such as on one rack.
+- **[MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/)** — dispatch workloads to other clusters with free capacity.
+- **[Elastic workloads](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/elastic_workload/)** — scale supported jobs up or down without suspending them.
+- **[Admission checks](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/)** — hold workloads until external checks pass, such as node capacity from cluster-autoscaler.
+- **[All-or-nothing (gang) scheduling](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/all_or_nothing/)** — by default, no Pods start until there is quota for all of them.
+- **[Flavor fungibility](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#flavorfungibility)** — choose when to try another [resource flavor](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/resource_flavor/), such as spot nodes, before borrowing or preempting.
+- **[Device quotas (DRA)](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/dynamic_resource_allocation/)** — admit workloads only when there is quota for the GPUs and other devices they request through DRA.
 
-```shell
-kubectl apply -f examples/admin/single-clusterqueue-setup.yaml
-```
+<details>
+<summary><strong>Integrations:</strong> Kubernetes Job, JobSet, KubeRay, LeaderWorkerSet and more</summary>
 
-Then you can run a job with:
+**Built-in integrations**, grouped by common use:
 
-```shell
-kubectl create -f examples/jobs/sample-job.yaml
-```
+- **Batch and training:** [Kubernetes Job](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/) (including [CronJobs](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/run_cronjobs/)), [JobSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobsets/), [MPIJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/mpijobs/), [RayJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayjobs/) ([KubeRay](https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/cluster/kubernetes/index.html)), [TrainJob (Kubeflow Trainer v2)](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/trainjobs/) and [SparkApplication](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/sparkapplications/) (alpha).
+- **Inference and serving:** [LeaderWorkerSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/leaderworkerset/), [RayService](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayservices/), [Deployment](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/deployment/) and [StatefulSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/statefulset/).
+- **Other workloads:** [Pods and Pod groups](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/plain_pods/), [AppWrapper](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/appwrappers/) and [RayCluster](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayclusters/).
+
+**External and custom integrations:** run [external frameworks](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/external_workloads/), such as [Argo Workflows](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/external_workloads/argo_workflow/) and [Tekton](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/external_workloads/tektoncd/), or [write an integration](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/dev/integrate_a_custom_job/) for your own workload types.
+
+**Deprecated:** [Kubeflow Trainer v1](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/) jobs ([PyTorchJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/pytorchjobs/), [TFJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/tfjobs/), [JAXJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/jaxjobs/), [PaddleJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/paddlejobs/), [XGBoostJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/xgboostjobs/)); migrate to [TrainJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/trainjobs/) or [JobSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobsets/).
 
-Learn more about:
-
-- Kueue [concepts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts).
-- Common and advanced [tasks](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks).
-
-## Roadmap
-
-High-level overview of the main priorities for 2026:
-- Improve user experience for [MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/) - multi-cluster Job dispatching, in particular:
-  * Support Elastic RayJob [#8712](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8712)
-  * Workload-Level Admission Constraints and Preference-Aware MultiKueue Dispatching [#8729](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8729)
-  * Prevent starting preemptions in multiple worker clusters [#8303](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8303)
-  * Support long running services [#8526](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8526)
-  * Log retrieval from worker clusters [#3526](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/3526)
-- Improve user experience for [Topology Aware Scheduling](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/topology_aware_scheduling/), in particular:
-  * Support for ResourceTransformations [#8860](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8860)
-  * Support for [Elastic Workloads](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/elastic_workload/) [#8160](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8160)
-  * Evict workloads which are running on nodes which become tainted [#8828](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8828)
-- Integration with the k8s native Workload-Aware Scheduler (WAS) and Topology-Aware Scheduling [#8871](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8871)
-- Support for Concurrent Workload Admission [#8691](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8691)
-- Support for running hero workloads [#8826](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8826)
+</details>
+
+<details>
+<summary><strong>Roadmap:</strong> priorities and long-term goals</summary>
+
+Priorities include integrating with Workload-Aware Scheduling (WAS) in kube-scheduler and continued improvements for training, inference and other workloads.
+
+**2026 priorities**
+
+- Integrate with Workload-Aware Scheduling (WAS) in kube-scheduler, including Topology-Aware Scheduling [#8871](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8871)
+- [MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/) improvements:
+  - Extend support for elastic Ray workloads, including autoscaling [#13243](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/13243) (replaces [#8712](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8712))
```

-# Kueue
-
-[![Latest Release](https://img.shields.io/github/v/release/kubernetes-sigs/kueue?include_prereleases)](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/releases/latest)
-[![Ask DeepWiki](https://deepwiki.com/badge.svg)](https://deepwiki.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue)
-
-<img src="https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/blob/main/site/static/images/logo.svg" width="100" alt="kueue logo">
-
-Kueue is a set of APIs and controller for [job](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload)
-[queueing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts#queueing). It is a job-level manager that decides when
-a job should be [admitted](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts#admission) to start (as in pods can be
-created) and when it should stop (as in active pods should be deleted).
-
-Read the [overview](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/overview/) and watch the Kueue-related [talks & presentations](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/community/talks_and_presentations/) to learn more.
-
-You can also [help Kueue grow](#help-kueue-grow).
-
-## Features overview
-
-- **Job management:** Support job queueing based on [priorities](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload/#priority) with different [strategies](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#queueing-strategy): `StrictFIFO` and `BestEffortFIFO`.
-- **Advanced Resource management:** Comprising: [resource flavor fungibility](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#flavorfungibility), [Fair Sharing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/preemption/#fair-sharing), [cohorts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#cohort) and [preemption](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#preemption) with a variety of policies between different tenants.
-- **Integrations:** Built-in support for popular jobs, e.g. [BatchJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/), [Kubeflow training jobs](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/), [RayJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayjobs/), [RayCluster](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayclusters/), [JobSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobsets/),  [plain Pod and Pod Groups](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/plain_pods/).
-- **System insight:** Built-in [prometheus metrics](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/reference/metrics/) to help monitor the state of the system, and on-demand visibility endpoint for [monitoring of pending workloads](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/manage/monitor_pending_workloads/pending_workloads_on_demand/).
-- **AdmissionChecks:** A mechanism for internal or external components to influence whether a workload can be [admitted](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/).
-- **Advanced autoscaling support:** Integration with cluster-autoscaler's [provisioningRequest](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/provisioning_request/#job-using-a-provisioningrequest) via admissionChecks.
-- **All-or-nothing with ready Pods:** A timeout-based implementation of [All-or-nothing scheduling](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/manage/setup_wait_for_pods_ready/).
-- **Partial admission and dynamic reclaim:** mechanisms to run a job with [reduced parallelism](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/#partial-admission), based on available quota, and to [release](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload/#dynamic-reclaim) the quota the pods complete.
-- **Mixing training and inference**: Simultaneous management of batch workloads along with serving workloads (such as [Deployments](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/deployment/) or [StatefulSets](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/statefulset/))
-- **Multi-cluster job dispatching:** called [MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/), allows to search for capacity and off-load the main cluster.
-- **Topology-Aware Scheduling**: Allows to optimize the Pod-to-Pod communication throughput by [scheduling aware of the data-center topology](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/topology_aware_scheduling/).
-- **Dynamic Resource Allocation (DRA):** Quota management for devices such as GPUs requested through [DRA](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/dynamic_resource_allocation/), either with ResourceClaimTemplates or extended resources, including partitionable devices. Consumable capacity is available as an alpha feature.
-

-
-## Installation
-
-**Officially supported and tested on Kubernetes 1.34 or newer**.
-
-To install the latest release of Kueue in your cluster, run the following command:
+<h1 align="center">
+  <img src="site/static/images/logo.svg" alt="Kueue logo" width="40" height="40"> Kueue
+</h1>
 
-```shell
-kubectl apply --server-side -f https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/releases/download/v0.20.3/manifests.yaml
-```
+<p align="center">
+  <strong><a href="#get-started">Get&nbsp;started</a></strong> ·
+  <a href="https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/">Docs</a> ·
+  <a href="#community">Community</a> ·
+  <a href="#help-kueue-grow">Help&nbsp;Kueue&nbsp;grow</a>
+</p>
 
-The controller runs in the `kueue-system` namespace.
+Kueue is a Kubernetes-native queueing and scheduling system that decides **when**, **where** and **how** your training, inference and batch workloads run.
 
-Read the [installation guide](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/installation/) to learn more.
+It works alongside kube-scheduler, cluster-autoscaler and your job controllers instead of replacing them.
+Learn more in the [overview](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/overview/).
 
-## Usage
+## Capabilities at a glance
 
-A minimal configuration can be set by running the [examples](site/static/examples):
+- **[Fair sharing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/fair_sharing/)** — share quota fairly across teams, with borrowing through [cohorts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cohort/) and optional preemption.
+- **[Topology-Aware Scheduling (TAS)](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/topology_aware_scheduling/)** — place communicating Pods close together, such as on one rack.
+- **[MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/)** — dispatch workloads to other clusters with free capacity.
+- **[Elastic workloads](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/elastic_workload/)** — scale supported jobs up or down without suspending them.
+- **[Admission checks](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/)** — hold workloads until external checks pass, such as node capacity from cluster-autoscaler.
+- **[All-or-nothing (gang) scheduling](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/all_or_nothing/)** — by default, no Pods start until there is quota for all of them.
+- **[Flavor fungibility](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#flavorfungibility)** — choose when to try another [resource flavor](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/resource_flavor/), such as spot nodes, before borrowing or preempting.
+- **[Device quotas (DRA)](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/dynamic_resource_allocation/)** — admit workloads only when there is quota for the GPUs and other devices they request through DRA.
 
-```shell
-kubectl apply -f examples/admin/single-clusterqueue-setup.yaml
-```
+<details>
+<summary><strong>Integrations:</strong> Kubernetes Job, JobSet, KubeRay, LeaderWorkerSet and more</summary>
 
-Then you can run a job with:
+**Built-in integrations**, grouped by common use:
 
-```shell
-kubectl create -f examples/jobs/sample-job.yaml
-```
+- **Batch and training:** [Kubernetes Job](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/) (including [CronJobs](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/run_cronjobs/)), [JobSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobsets/), [MPIJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/mpijobs/), [RayJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayjobs/) ([KubeRay](https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/cluster/kubernetes/index.html)), [TrainJob (Kubeflow Trainer v2)](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/trainjobs/) and [SparkApplication](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/sparkapplications/) (alpha).
+- **Inference and serving:** [LeaderWorkerSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/leaderworkerset/), [RayService](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayservices/), [Deployment](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/deployment/) and [StatefulSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/statefulset/).
+- **Other workloads:** [Pods and Pod groups](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/plain_pods/), [AppWrapper](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/appwrappers/) and [RayCluster](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayclusters/).
+
+**External and custom integrations:** run [external frameworks](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/external_workloads/), such as [Argo Workflows](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/external_workloads/argo_workflow/) and [Tekton](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/external_workloads/tektoncd/), or [write an integration](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/dev/integrate_a_custom_job/) for your own workload types.
+
+**Deprecated:** [Kubeflow Trainer v1](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/) jobs ([PyTorchJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/pytorchjobs/), [TFJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/tfjobs/), [JAXJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/jaxjobs/), [PaddleJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/paddlejobs/), [XGBoostJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/xgboostjobs/)); migrate to [TrainJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/trainjobs/) or [JobSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobsets/).
 
-Learn more about:
-
-- Kueue [concepts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts).
-- Common and advanced [tasks](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks).
-
-## Roadmap
-
-High-level overview of the main priorities for 2026:
-- Improve user experience for [MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/) - multi-cluster Job dispatching, in particular:
-  * Support Elastic RayJob [#8712](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8712)
-  * Workload-Level Admission Constraints and Preference-Aware MultiKueue Dispatching [#8729](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8729)
-  * Prevent starting preemptions in multiple worker clusters [#8303](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8303)
-  * Support long running services [#8526](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8526)
-  * Log retrieval from worker clusters [#3526](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/3526)
-- Improve user experience for [Topology Aware Scheduling](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/topology_aware_scheduling/), in particular:
-  * Support for ResourceTransformations [#8860](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8860)
-  * Support for [Elastic Workloads](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/elastic_workload/) [#8160](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8160)
-  * Evict workloads which are running on nodes which become tainted [#8828](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8828)
-- Integration with the k8s native Workload-Aware Scheduler (WAS) and Topology-Aware Scheduling [#8871](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8871)
-- Support for Concurrent Workload Admission [#8691](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8691)
-- Support for running hero workloads [#8826](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8826)
+</details>
+
+<details>
+<summary><strong>Roadmap:</strong> priorities and long-term goals</summary>
+
+Priorities include integrating with Workload-Aware Scheduling (WAS) in kube-scheduler and continued improvements for training, inference and other workloads.
+
+**2026 priorities**
+
+- Integrate with Workload-Aware Scheduling (WAS) in kube-scheduler, including Topology-Aware Scheduling [#8871](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8871)
+- [MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/) improvements:
+  - Extend support for elastic Ray workloads, including autoscaling [#13243](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/13243) (replaces [#8712](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8712))
```

-## Features overview
-
-- **Job management:** Support job queueing based on [priorities](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload/#priority) with different [strategies](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#queueing-strategy): `StrictFIFO` and `BestEffortFIFO`.
-- **Advanced Resource management:** Comprising: [resource flavor fungibility](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#flavorfungibility), [Fair Sharing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/preemption/#fair-sharing), [cohorts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#cohort) and [preemption](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#preemption) with a variety of policies between different tenants.
-- **Integrations:** Built-in support for popular jobs, e.g. [BatchJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/), [Kubeflow training jobs](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/), [RayJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayjobs/), [RayCluster](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayclusters/), [JobSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobsets/),  [plain Pod and Pod Groups](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/plain_pods/).
-- **System insight:** Built-in [prometheus metrics](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/reference/metrics/) to help monitor the state of the system, and on-demand visibility endpoint for [monitoring of pending workloads](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/manage/monitor_pending_workloads/pending_workloads_on_demand/).
-- **AdmissionChecks:** A mechanism for internal or external components to influence whether a workload can be [admitted](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/).
-- **Advanced autoscaling support:** Integration with cluster-autoscaler's [provisioningRequest](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/provisioning_request/#job-using-a-provisioningrequest) via admissionChecks.
-- **All-or-nothing with ready Pods:** A timeout-based implementation of [All-or-nothing scheduling](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/manage/setup_wait_for_pods_ready/).
-- **Partial admission and dynamic reclaim:** mechanisms to run a job with [reduced parallelism](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/#partial-admission), based on available quota, and to [release](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload/#dynamic-reclaim) the quota the pods complete.
-- **Mixing training and inference**: Simultaneous management of batch workloads along with serving workloads (such as [Deployments](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/deployment/) or [StatefulSets](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/statefulset/))
-- **Multi-cluster job dispatching:** called [MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/), allows to search for capacity and off-load the main cluster.
-- **Topology-Aware Scheduling**: Allows to optimize the Pod-to-Pod communication throughput by [scheduling aware of the data-center topology](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/topology_aware_scheduling/).
-- **Dynamic Resource Allocation (DRA):** Quota management for devices such as GPUs requested through [DRA](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/dynamic_resource_allocation/), either with ResourceClaimTemplates or extended resources, including partitionable devices. Consumable capacity is available as an alpha feature.
-

-  address new use cases_.
-
-## Installation
-
-**Officially supported and tested on Kubernetes 1.34 or newer**.
-
-To install the latest release of Kueue in your cluster, run the following command:
+<h1 align="center">
+  <img src="site/static/images/logo.svg" alt="Kueue logo" width="40" height="40"> Kueue
+</h1>
 
-```shell
-kubectl apply --server-side -f https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/releases/download/v0.20.3/manifests.yaml
-```
+<p align="center">
+  <strong><a href="#get-started">Get&nbsp;started</a></strong> ·
+  <a href="https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/">Docs</a> ·
+  <a href="#community">Community</a> ·
+  <a href="#help-kueue-grow">Help&nbsp;Kueue&nbsp;grow</a>
+</p>
 
-The controller runs in the `kueue-system` namespace.
+Kueue is a Kubernetes-native queueing and scheduling system that decides **when**, **where** and **how** your training, inference and batch workloads run.
 
-Read the [installation guide](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/installation/) to learn more.
+It works alongside kube-scheduler, cluster-autoscaler and your job controllers instead of replacing them.
+Learn more in the [overview](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/overview/).
 
-## Usage
+## Capabilities at a glance
 
-A minimal configuration can be set by running the [examples](site/static/examples):
+- **[Fair sharing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/fair_sharing/)** — share quota fairly across teams, with borrowing through [cohorts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cohort/) and optional preemption.
+- **[Topology-Aware Scheduling (TAS)](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/topology_aware_scheduling/)** — place communicating Pods close together, such as on one rack.
+- **[MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/)** — dispatch workloads to other clusters with free capacity.
+- **[Elastic workloads](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/elastic_workload/)** — scale supported jobs up or down without suspending them.
+- **[Admission checks](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/)** — hold workloads until external checks pass, such as node capacity from cluster-autoscaler.
+- **[All-or-nothing (gang) scheduling](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/all_or_nothing/)** — by default, no Pods start until there is quota for all of them.
+- **[Flavor fungibility](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#flavorfungibility)** — choose when to try another [resource flavor](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/resource_flavor/), such as spot nodes, before borrowing or preempting.
+- **[Device quotas (DRA)](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/dynamic_resource_allocation/)** — admit workloads only when there is quota for the GPUs and other devices they request through DRA.
 
-```shell
-kubectl apply -f examples/admin/single-clusterqueue-setup.yaml
-```
+<details>
+<summary><strong>Integrations:</strong> Kubernetes Job, JobSet, KubeRay, LeaderWorkerSet and more</summary>
 
-Then you can run a job with:
+**Built-in integrations**, grouped by common use:
 
-```shell
-kubectl create -f examples/jobs/sample-job.yaml
-```
+- **Batch and training:** [Kubernetes Job](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/) (including [CronJobs](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/run_cronjobs/)), [JobSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobsets/), [MPIJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/mpijobs/), [RayJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayjobs/) ([KubeRay](https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/cluster/kubernetes/index.html)), [TrainJob (Kubeflow Trainer v2)](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/trainjobs/) and [SparkApplication](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/sparkapplications/) (alpha).
+- **Inference and serving:** [LeaderWorkerSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/leaderworkerset/), [RayService](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayservices/), [Deployment](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/deployment/) and [StatefulSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/statefulset/).
+- **Other workloads:** [Pods and Pod groups](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/plain_pods/), [AppWrapper](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/appwrappers/) and [RayCluster](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayclusters/).
+
+**External and custom integrations:** run [external frameworks](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/external_workloads/), such as [Argo Workflows](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/external_workloads/argo_workflow/) and [Tekton](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/external_workloads/tektoncd/), or [write an integration](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/dev/integrate_a_custom_job/) for your own workload types.
+
+**Deprecated:** [Kubeflow Trainer v1](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/) jobs ([PyTorchJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/pytorchjobs/), [TFJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/tfjobs/), [JAXJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/jaxjobs/), [PaddleJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/paddlejobs/), [XGBoostJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/xgboostjobs/)); migrate to [TrainJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/trainjobs/) or [JobSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobsets/).
 
-Learn more about:
-
-- Kueue [concepts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts).
-- Common and advanced [tasks](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks).
-
-## Roadmap
-
-High-level overview of the main priorities for 2026:
-- Improve user experience for [MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/) - multi-cluster Job dispatching, in particular:
-  * Support Elastic RayJob [#8712](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8712)
-  * Workload-Level Admission Constraints and Preference-Aware MultiKueue Dispatching [#8729](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8729)
-  * Prevent starting preemptions in multiple worker clusters [#8303](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8303)
-  * Support long running services [#8526](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8526)
-  * Log retrieval from worker clusters [#3526](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/3526)
-- Improve user experience for [Topology Aware Scheduling](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/topology_aware_scheduling/), in particular:
-  * Support for ResourceTransformations [#8860](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8860)
-  * Support for [Elastic Workloads](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/elastic_workload/) [#8160](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8160)
-  * Evict workloads which are running on nodes which become tainted [#8828](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8828)
-- Integration with the k8s native Workload-Aware Scheduler (WAS) and Topology-Aware Scheduling [#8871](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8871)
-- Support for Concurrent Workload Admission [#8691](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8691)
-- Support for running hero workloads [#8826](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8826)
+</details>
+
+<details>
+<summary><strong>Roadmap:</strong> priorities and long-term goals</summary>
+
+Priorities include integrating with Workload-Aware Scheduling (WAS) in kube-scheduler and continued improvements for training, inference and other workloads.
+
+**2026 priorities**
+
+- Integrate with Workload-Aware Scheduling (WAS) in kube-scheduler, including Topology-Aware Scheduling [#8871](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8871)
+- [MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/) improvements:
+  - Extend support for elastic Ray workloads, including autoscaling [#13243](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/13243) (replaces [#8712](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8712))
```

-# Kueue
-
-[![Latest Release](https://img.shields.io/github/v/release/kubernetes-sigs/kueue?include_prereleases)](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/releases/latest)
-[![Ask DeepWiki](https://deepwiki.com/badge.svg)](https://deepwiki.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue)
-
-<img src="https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/blob/main/site/static/images/logo.svg" width="100" alt="kueue logo">
-
-Kueue is a set of APIs and controller for [job](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload)
-[queueing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts#queueing). It is a job-level manager that decides when
-a job should be [admitted](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts#admission) to start (as in pods can be
-created) and when it should stop (as in active pods should be deleted).
-
-Read the [overview](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/overview/) and watch the Kueue-related [talks & presentations](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/community/talks_and_presentations/) to learn more.
-
-You can also [help Kueue grow](#help-kueue-grow).
-
-## Features overview
-
-- **Job management:** Support job queueing based on [priorities](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload/#priority) with different [strategies](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#queueing-strategy): `StrictFIFO` and `BestEffortFIFO`.
-- **Advanced Resource management:** Comprising: [resource flavor fungibility](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#flavorfungibility), [Fair Sharing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/preemption/#fair-sharing), [cohorts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#cohort) and [preemption](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#preemption) with a variety of policies between different tenants.
-- **Integrations:** Built-in support for popular jobs, e.g. [BatchJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/), [Kubeflow training jobs](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/), [RayJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayjobs/), [RayCluster](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayclusters/), [JobSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobsets/),  [plain Pod and Pod Groups](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/plain_pods/).
-- **System insight:** Built-in [prometheus metrics](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/reference/metrics/) to help monitor the state of the system, and on-demand visibility endpoint for [monitoring of pending workloads](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/manage/monitor_pending_workloads/pending_workloads_on_demand/).
-- **AdmissionChecks:** A mechanism for internal or external components to influence whether a workload can be [admitted](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/).
-- **Advanced autoscaling support:** Integration with cluster-autoscaler's [provisioningRequest](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/provisioning_request/#job-using-a-provisioningrequest) via admissionChecks.
-- **All-or-nothing with ready Pods:** A timeout-based implementation of [All-or-nothing scheduling](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/manage/setup_wait_for_pods_ready/).
-- **Partial admission and dynamic reclaim:** mechanisms to run a job with [reduced parallelism](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/#partial-admission), based on available quota, and to [release](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload/#dynamic-reclaim) the quota the pods complete.
-- **Mixing training and inference**: Simultaneous management of batch workloads along with serving workloads (such as [Deployments](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/deployment/) or [StatefulSets](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/statefulset/))
-- **Multi-cluster job dispatching:** called [MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/), allows to search for capacity and off-load the main cluster.
-- **Topology-Aware Scheduling**: Allows to optimize the Pod-to-Pod communication throughput by [scheduling aware of the data-center topology](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/topology_aware_scheduling/).
-- **Dynamic Resource Allocation (DRA):** Quota management for devices such as GPUs requested through [DRA](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/dynamic_resource_allocation/), either with ResourceClaimTemplates or extended resources, including partitionable devices. Consumable capacity is available as an alpha feature.
-

-  _Based on community feedback, we continue to simplify and evolve the API to
-  address new use cases_.
-
-## Installation
-
-**Officially supported and tested on Kubernetes 1.34 or newer**.
-
-To install the latest release of Kueue in your cluster, run the following command:
+<h1 align="center">
+  <img src="site/static/images/logo.svg" alt="Kueue logo" width="40" height="40"> Kueue
+</h1>
 
-```shell
-kubectl apply --server-side -f https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/releases/download/v0.20.3/manifests.yaml
-```
+<p align="center">
+  <strong><a href="#get-started">Get&nbsp;started</a></strong> ·
+  <a href="https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/">Docs</a> ·
+  <a href="#community">Community</a> ·
+  <a href="#help-kueue-grow">Help&nbsp;Kueue&nbsp;grow</a>
+</p>
 
-The controller runs in the `kueue-system` namespace.
+Kueue is a Kubernetes-native queueing and scheduling system that decides **when**, **where** and **how** your training, inference and batch workloads run.
 
-Read the [installation guide](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/installation/) to learn more.
+It works alongside kube-scheduler, cluster-autoscaler and your job controllers instead of replacing them.
+Learn more in the [overview](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/overview/).
 
-## Usage
+## Capabilities at a glance
 
-A minimal configuration can be set by running the [examples](site/static/examples):
+- **[Fair sharing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/fair_sharing/)** — share quota fairly across teams, with borrowing through [cohorts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cohort/) and optional preemption.
+- **[Topology-Aware Scheduling (TAS)](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/topology_aware_scheduling/)** — place communicating Pods close together, such as on one rack.
+- **[MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/)** — dispatch workloads to other clusters with free capacity.
+- **[Elastic workloads](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/elastic_workload/)** — scale supported jobs up or down without suspending them.
+- **[Admission checks](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/)** — hold workloads until external checks pass, such as node capacity from cluster-autoscaler.
+- **[All-or-nothing (gang) scheduling](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/all_or_nothing/)** — by default, no Pods start until there is quota for all of them.
+- **[Flavor fungibility](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#flavorfungibility)** — choose when to try another [resource flavor](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/resource_flavor/), such as spot nodes, before borrowing or preempting.
+- **[Device quotas (DRA)](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/dynamic_resource_allocation/)** — admit workloads only when there is quota for the GPUs and other devices they request through DRA.
 
-```shell
-kubectl apply -f examples/admin/single-clusterqueue-setup.yaml
-```
+<details>
+<summary><strong>Integrations:</strong> Kubernetes Job, JobSet, KubeRay, LeaderWorkerSet and more</summary>
 
-Then you can run a job with:
+**Built-in integrations**, grouped by common use:
 
-```shell
-kubectl create -f examples/jobs/sample-job.yaml
-```
+- **Batch and training:** [Kubernetes Job](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/) (including [CronJobs](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/run_cronjobs/)), [JobSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobsets/), [MPIJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/mpijobs/), [RayJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayjobs/) ([KubeRay](https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/cluster/kubernetes/index.html)), [TrainJob (Kubeflow Trainer v2)](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/trainjobs/) and [SparkApplication](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/sparkapplications/) (alpha).
+- **Inference and serving:** [LeaderWorkerSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/leaderworkerset/), [RayService](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayservices/), [Deployment](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/deployment/) and [StatefulSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/statefulset/).
+- **Other workloads:** [Pods and Pod groups](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/plain_pods/), [AppWrapper](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/appwrappers/) and [RayCluster](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayclusters/).
+
+**External and custom integrations:** run [external frameworks](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/external_workloads/), such as [Argo Workflows](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/external_workloads/argo_workflow/) and [Tekton](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/external_workloads/tektoncd/), or [write an integration](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/dev/integrate_a_custom_job/) for your own workload types.
+
+**Deprecated:** [Kubeflow Trainer v1](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/) jobs ([PyTorchJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/pytorchjobs/), [TFJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/tfjobs/), [JAXJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/jaxjobs/), [PaddleJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/paddlejobs/), [XGBoostJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/xgboostjobs/)); migrate to [TrainJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/trainjobs/) or [JobSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobsets/).
 
-Learn more about:
-
-- Kueue [concepts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts).
-- Common and advanced [tasks](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks).
-
-## Roadmap
-
-High-level overview of the main priorities for 2026:
-- Improve user experience for [MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/) - multi-cluster Job dispatching, in particular:
-  * Support Elastic RayJob [#8712](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8712)
-  * Workload-Level Admission Constraints and Preference-Aware MultiKueue Dispatching [#8729](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8729)
-  * Prevent starting preemptions in multiple worker clusters [#8303](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8303)
-  * Support long running services [#8526](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8526)
-  * Log retrieval from worker clusters [#3526](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/3526)
-- Improve user experience for [Topology Aware Scheduling](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/topology_aware_scheduling/), in particular:
-  * Support for ResourceTransformations [#8860](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8860)
-  * Support for [Elastic Workloads](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/elastic_workload/) [#8160](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8160)
-  * Evict workloads which are running on nodes which become tainted [#8828](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8828)
-- Integration with the k8s native Workload-Aware Scheduler (WAS) and Topology-Aware Scheduling [#8871](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8871)
-- Support for Concurrent Workload Admission [#8691](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8691)
-- Support for running hero workloads [#8826](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8826)
+</details>
+
+<details>
+<summary><strong>Roadmap:</strong> priorities and long-term goals</summary>
+
+Priorities include integrating with Workload-Aware Scheduling (WAS) in kube-scheduler and continued improvements for training, inference and other workloads.
+
+**2026 priorities**
+
+- Integrate with Workload-Aware Scheduling (WAS) in kube-scheduler, including Topology-Aware Scheduling [#8871](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8871)
+- [MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/) improvements:
+  - Extend support for elastic Ray workloads, including autoscaling [#13243](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/13243) (replaces [#8712](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8712))
```

-# Kueue
-
-[![Latest Release](https://img.shields.io/github/v/release/kubernetes-sigs/kueue?include_prereleases)](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/releases/latest)
-[![Ask DeepWiki](https://deepwiki.com/badge.svg)](https://deepwiki.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue)
-
-<img src="https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/blob/main/site/static/images/logo.svg" width="100" alt="kueue logo">
-
-Kueue is a set of APIs and controller for [job](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload)
-[queueing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts#queueing). It is a job-level manager that decides when
-a job should be [admitted](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts#admission) to start (as in pods can be
-created) and when it should stop (as in active pods should be deleted).
-
-Read the [overview](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/overview/) and watch the Kueue-related [talks & presentations](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/community/talks_and_presentations/) to learn more.
-
-You can also [help Kueue grow](#help-kueue-grow).
-
-## Features overview
-
-- **Job management:** Support job queueing based on [priorities](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload/#priority) with different [strategies](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#queueing-strategy): `StrictFIFO` and `BestEffortFIFO`.
-- **Advanced Resource management:** Comprising: [resource flavor fungibility](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#flavorfungibility), [Fair Sharing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/preemption/#fair-sharing), [cohorts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#cohort) and [preemption](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#preemption) with a variety of policies between different tenants.
-- **Integrations:** Built-in support for popular jobs, e.g. [BatchJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/), [Kubeflow training jobs](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/), [RayJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayjobs/), [RayCluster](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayclusters/), [JobSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobsets/),  [plain Pod and Pod Groups](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/plain_pods/).
-- **System insight:** Built-in [prometheus metrics](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/reference/metrics/) to help monitor the state of the system, and on-demand visibility endpoint for [monitoring of pending workloads](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/manage/monitor_pending_workloads/pending_workloads_on_demand/).
-- **AdmissionChecks:** A mechanism for internal or external components to influence whether a workload can be [admitted](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/).
-- **Advanced autoscaling support:** Integration with cluster-autoscaler's [provisioningRequest](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/provisioning_request/#job-using-a-provisioningrequest) via admissionChecks.
-- **All-or-nothing with ready Pods:** A timeout-based implementation of [All-or-nothing scheduling](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/manage/setup_wait_for_pods_ready/).
-- **Partial admission and dynamic reclaim:** mechanisms to run a job with [reduced parallelism](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/#partial-admission), based on available quota, and to [release](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload/#dynamic-reclaim) the quota the pods complete.
-- **Mixing training and inference**: Simultaneous management of batch workloads along with serving workloads (such as [Deployments](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/deployment/) or [StatefulSets](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/statefulset/))
-- **Multi-cluster job dispatching:** called [MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/), allows to search for capacity and off-load the main cluster.
-- **Topology-Aware Scheduling**: Allows to optimize the Pod-to-Pod communication throughput by [scheduling aware of the data-center topology](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/topology_aware_scheduling/).
-- **Dynamic Resource Allocation (DRA):** Quota management for devices such as GPUs requested through [DRA](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/dynamic_resource_allocation/), either with ResourceClaimTemplates or extended resources, including partitionable devices. Consumable capacity is available as an alpha feature.
-

-  _Based on community feedback, we continue to simplify and evolve the API to
-  address new use cases_.
-
-## Installation
-
-**Officially supported and tested on Kubernetes 1.34 or newer**.
-
-To install the latest release of Kueue in your cluster, run the following command:
+<h1 align="center">
+  <img src="site/static/images/logo.svg" alt="Kueue logo" width="40" height="40"> Kueue
+</h1>
 
-```shell
-kubectl apply --server-side -f https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/releases/download/v0.20.3/manifests.yaml
-```
+<p align="center">
+  <strong><a href="#get-started">Get&nbsp;started</a></strong> ·
+  <a href="https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/">Docs</a> ·
+  <a href="#community">Community</a> ·
+  <a href="#help-kueue-grow">Help&nbsp;Kueue&nbsp;grow</a>
+</p>
 
-The controller runs in the `kueue-system` namespace.
+Kueue is a Kubernetes-native queueing and scheduling system that decides **when**, **where** and **how** your training, inference and batch workloads run.
 
-Read the [installation guide](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/installation/) to learn more.
+It works alongside kube-scheduler, cluster-autoscaler and your job controllers instead of replacing them.
+Learn more in the [overview](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/overview/).
 
-## Usage
+## Capabilities at a glance
 
-A minimal configuration can be set by running the [examples](site/static/examples):
+- **[Fair sharing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/fair_sharing/)** — share quota fairly across teams, with borrowing through [cohorts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cohort/) and optional preemption.
+- **[Topology-Aware Scheduling (TAS)](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/topology_aware_scheduling/)** — place communicating Pods close together, such as on one rack.
+- **[MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/)** — dispatch workloads to other clusters with free capacity.
+- **[Elastic workloads](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/elastic_workload/)** — scale supported jobs up or down without suspending them.
+- **[Admission checks](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/)** — hold workloads until external checks pass, such as node capacity from cluster-autoscaler.
+- **[All-or-nothing (gang) scheduling](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/all_or_nothing/)** — by default, no Pods start until there is quota for all of them.
+- **[Flavor fungibility](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#flavorfungibility)** — choose when to try another [resource flavor](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/resource_flavor/), such as spot nodes, before borrowing or preempting.
+- **[Device quotas (DRA)](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/dynamic_resource_allocation/)** — admit workloads only when there is quota for the GPUs and other devices they request through DRA.
 
-```shell
-kubectl apply -f examples/admin/single-clusterqueue-setup.yaml
-```
+<details>
+<summary><strong>Integrations:</strong> Kubernetes Job, JobSet, KubeRay, LeaderWorkerSet and more</summary>
 
-Then you can run a job with:
+**Built-in integrations**, grouped by common use:
 
-```shell
-kubectl create -f examples/jobs/sample-job.yaml
-```
+- **Batch and training:** [Kubernetes Job](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/) (including [CronJobs](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/run_cronjobs/)), [JobSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobsets/), [MPIJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/mpijobs/), [RayJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayjobs/) ([KubeRay](https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/cluster/kubernetes/index.html)), [TrainJob (Kubeflow Trainer v2)](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/trainjobs/) and [SparkApplication](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/sparkapplications/) (alpha).
+- **Inference and serving:** [LeaderWorkerSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/leaderworkerset/), [RayService](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayservices/), [Deployment](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/deployment/) and [StatefulSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/statefulset/).
+- **Other workloads:** [Pods and Pod groups](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/plain_pods/), [AppWrapper](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/appwrappers/) and [RayCluster](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayclusters/).
+
+**External and custom integrations:** run [external frameworks](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/external_workloads/), such as [Argo Workflows](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/external_workloads/argo_workflow/) and [Tekton](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/external_workloads/tektoncd/), or [write an integration](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/dev/integrate_a_custom_job/) for your own workload types.
+
+**Deprecated:** [Kubeflow Trainer v1](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/) jobs ([PyTorchJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/pytorchjobs/), [TFJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/tfjobs/), [JAXJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/jaxjobs/), [PaddleJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/paddlejobs/), [XGBoostJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/xgboostjobs/)); migrate to [TrainJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/trainjobs/) or [JobSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobsets/).
 
-Learn more about:
-
-- Kueue [concepts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts).
-- Common and advanced [tasks](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks).
-
-## Roadmap
-
-High-level overview of the main priorities for 2026:
-- Improve user experience for [MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/) - multi-cluster Job dispatching, in particular:
-  * Support Elastic RayJob [#8712](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8712)
-  * Workload-Level Admission Constraints and Preference-Aware MultiKueue Dispatching [#8729](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8729)
-  * Prevent starting preemptions in multiple worker clusters [#8303](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8303)
-  * Support long running services [#8526](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8526)
-  * Log retrieval from worker clusters [#3526](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/3526)
-- Improve user experience for [Topology Aware Scheduling](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/topology_aware_scheduling/), in particular:
-  * Support for ResourceTransformations [#8860](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8860)
-  * Support for [Elastic Workloads](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/elastic_workload/) [#8160](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8160)
-  * Evict workloads which are running on nodes which become tainted [#8828](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8828)
-- Integration with the k8s native Workload-Aware Scheduler (WAS) and Topology-Aware Scheduling [#8871](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8871)
-- Support for Concurrent Workload Admission [#8691](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8691)
-- Support for running hero workloads [#8826](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8826)
+</details>
+
+<details>
+<summary><strong>Roadmap:</strong> priorities and long-term goals</summary>
+
+Priorities include integrating with Workload-Aware Scheduling (WAS) in kube-scheduler and continued improvements for training, inference and other workloads.
+
+**2026 priorities**
+
+- Integrate with Workload-Aware Scheduling (WAS) in kube-scheduler, including Topology-Aware Scheduling [#8871](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8871)
+- [MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/) improvements:
+  - Extend support for elastic Ray workloads, including autoscaling [#13243](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/13243) (replaces [#8712](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8712))
```

-# Kueue
-
-[![Latest Release](https://img.shields.io/github/v/release/kubernetes-sigs/kueue?include_prereleases)](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/releases/latest)
-[![Ask DeepWiki](https://deepwiki.com/badge.svg)](https://deepwiki.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue)
-
-<img src="https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/blob/main/site/static/images/logo.svg" width="100" alt="kueue logo">
-
-Kueue is a set of APIs and controller for [job](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload)
-[queueing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts#queueing). It is a job-level manager that decides when
-a job should be [admitted](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts#admission) to start (as in pods can be
-created) and when it should stop (as in active pods should be deleted).
-
-Read the [overview](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/overview/) and watch the Kueue-related [talks & presentations](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/community/talks_and_presentations/) to learn more.
-
-You can also [help Kueue grow](#help-kueue-grow).
-
-## Features overview
-
-- **Job management:** Support job queueing based on [priorities](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload/#priority) with different [strategies](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#queueing-strategy): `StrictFIFO` and `BestEffortFIFO`.
-- **Advanced Resource management:** Comprising: [resource flavor fungibility](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#flavorfungibility), [Fair Sharing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/preemption/#fair-sharing), [cohorts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#cohort) and [preemption](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#preemption) with a variety of policies between different tenants.
-- **Integrations:** Built-in support for popular jobs, e.g. [BatchJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/), [Kubeflow training jobs](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/), [RayJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayjobs/), [RayCluster](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayclusters/), [JobSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobsets/),  [plain Pod and Pod Groups](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/plain_pods/).
-- **System insight:** Built-in [prometheus metrics](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/reference/metrics/) to help monitor the state of the system, and on-demand visibility endpoint for [monitoring of pending workloads](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/manage/monitor_pending_workloads/pending_workloads_on_demand/).
-- **AdmissionChecks:** A mechanism for internal or external components to influence whether a workload can be [admitted](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/).
-- **Advanced autoscaling support:** Integration with cluster-autoscaler's [provisioningRequest](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/provisioning_request/#job-using-a-provisioningrequest) via admissionChecks.
-- **All-or-nothing with ready Pods:** A timeout-based implementation of [All-or-nothing scheduling](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/manage/setup_wait_for_pods_ready/).
-- **Partial admission and dynamic reclaim:** mechanisms to run a job with [reduced parallelism](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/#partial-admission), based on available quota, and to [release](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload/#dynamic-reclaim) the quota the pods complete.
-- **Mixing training and inference**: Simultaneous management of batch workloads along with serving workloads (such as [Deployments](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/deployment/) or [StatefulSets](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/statefulset/))
-- **Multi-cluster job dispatching:** called [MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/), allows to search for capacity and off-load the main cluster.
-- **Topology-Aware Scheduling**: Allows to optimize the Pod-to-Pod communication throughput by [scheduling aware of the data-center topology](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/topology_aware_scheduling/).
-- **Dynamic Resource Allocation (DRA):** Quota management for devices such as GPUs requested through [DRA](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/dynamic_resource_allocation/), either with ResourceClaimTemplates or extended resources, including partitionable devices. Consumable capacity is available as an alpha feature.
-

-## Installation
-
-**Officially supported and tested on Kubernetes 1.34 or newer**.
-
-To install the latest release of Kueue in your cluster, run the following command:
+<h1 align="center">
+  <img src="site/static/images/logo.svg" alt="Kueue logo" width="40" height="40"> Kueue
+</h1>
 
-```shell
-kubectl apply --server-side -f https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/releases/download/v0.20.3/manifests.yaml
-```
+<p align="center">
+  <strong><a href="#get-started">Get&nbsp;started</a></strong> ·
+  <a href="https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/">Docs</a> ·
+  <a href="#community">Community</a> ·
+  <a href="#help-kueue-grow">Help&nbsp;Kueue&nbsp;grow</a>
+</p>
 
-The controller runs in the `kueue-system` namespace.
+Kueue is a Kubernetes-native queueing and scheduling system that decides **when**, **where** and **how** your training, inference and batch workloads run.
 
-Read the [installation guide](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/installation/) to learn more.
+It works alongside kube-scheduler, cluster-autoscaler and your job controllers instead of replacing them.
+Learn more in the [overview](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/overview/).
 
-## Usage
+## Capabilities at a glance
 
-A minimal configuration can be set by running the [examples](site/static/examples):
+- **[Fair sharing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/fair_sharing/)** — share quota fairly across teams, with borrowing through [cohorts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cohort/) and optional preemption.
```

  Maybe organize the capabilities into topic or in some order? Right now they are quite shuffled.

- **README.md:109** by [@mwielgus](https://github.com/mwielgus) — 2026-10-26T10:29:32Z

@@ -1,194 +1,155 @@
-# Kueue
-
-[![Latest Release](https://img.shields.io/github/v/release/kubernetes-sigs/kueue?include_prereleases)](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/releases/latest)
-[![Ask DeepWiki](https://deepwiki.com/badge.svg)](https://deepwiki.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue)
-
-<img src="https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/blob/main/site/static/images/logo.svg" width="100" alt="kueue logo">
-
-Kueue is a set of APIs and controller for [job](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload)
-[queueing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts#queueing). It is a job-level manager that decides when
-a job should be [admitted](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts#admission) to start (as in pods can be
-created) and when it should stop (as in active pods should be deleted).
-
-Read the [overview](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/overview/) and watch the Kueue-related [talks & presentations](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/community/talks_and_presentations/) to learn more.
-
-You can also [help Kueue grow](#help-kueue-grow).
-
-## Features overview
-
-- **Job management:** Support job queueing based on [priorities](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload/#priority) with different [strategies](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#queueing-strategy): `StrictFIFO` and `BestEffortFIFO`.
-- **Advanced Resource management:** Comprising: [resource flavor fungibility](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#flavorfungibility), [Fair Sharing](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/preemption/#fair-sharing), [cohorts](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#cohort) and [preemption](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/cluster_queue/#preemption) with a variety of policies between different tenants.
-- **Integrations:** Built-in support for popular jobs, e.g. [BatchJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/), [Kubeflow training jobs](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/kubeflow/), [RayJob](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayjobs/), [RayCluster](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/rayclusters/), [JobSet](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobsets/),  [plain Pod and Pod Groups](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/plain_pods/).
-- **System insight:** Built-in [prometheus metrics](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/reference/metrics/) to help monitor the state of the system, and on-demand visibility endpoint for [monitoring of pending workloads](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/manage/monitor_pending_workloads/pending_workloads_on_demand/).
-- **AdmissionChecks:** A mechanism for internal or external components to influence whether a workload can be [admitted](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/).
-- **Advanced autoscaling support:** Integration with cluster-autoscaler's [provisioningRequest](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/admission_check/provisioning_request/#job-using-a-provisioningrequest) via admissionChecks.
-- **All-or-nothing with ready Pods:** A timeout-based implementation of [All-or-nothing scheduling](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/manage/setup_wait_for_pods_ready/).
-- **Partial admission and dynamic reclaim:** mechanisms to run a job with [reduced parallelism](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/jobs/#partial-admission), based on available quota, and to [release](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/workload/#dynamic-reclaim) the quota the pods complete.
-- **Mixing training and inference**: Simultaneous management of batch workloads along with serving workloads (such as [Deployments](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/deployment/) or [StatefulSets](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/tasks/run/statefulset/))
-- **Multi-cluster job dispatching:** called [MultiKueue](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/multikueue/), allows to search for capacity and off-load the main cluster.
-- **Topology-Aware Scheduling**: Allows to optimize the Pod-to-Pod communication throughput by [scheduling aware of the data-center topology](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/topology_aware_scheduling/).
-- **Dynamic Resource Allocation (DRA):** Quota management for devices such as GPUs requested through [DRA](https://kueue.sigs.k8s.io/docs/concepts/dynamic_resource_allocation/), either with ResourceClaimTemplates or extended resources, including partitionable devices. Consumable capacity is available as an alpha feature.
-
